- **hdfs**: HDFS 相关技能的入口。当问题提到 HDFS、丢块（BlockMissingException）、集群存储满（Could not get block locations / Unable to close file because the last）、存储集群负载、DataNode、NameNode、目录列表（ls）、路径统计（count）、创建目录（mkdir）、上传文件（put）等 HDFS 相关问题时，使用此技能了解可用的子技能列表并路由到合适的技能。 ([TunoC2C/workbuddy-experts](https://github.com/TunoC2C/workbuddy-experts/tree/HEAD/plugins/ab-experiment-analyst/skills/tencent-bigdata/sub-skills/HDFS) · ★1)
- **hdfs-basic-operations**: 当用户需要执行 HDFS 基础操作时使用此 skill，包括查看目录内容（ls）、路径统计（count）、磁盘空间（du）、文件状态（stat）、路径检测（test）、文件上传（put），通过 do-bigdata CLI 调用 do_mcp API 服务执行 HDFS 命令，每个操作对应独立的 CLI 子命令。 ([TunoC2C/workbuddy-experts](https://github.com/TunoC2C/workbuddy-experts/tree/HEAD/plugins/ab-experiment-analyst/skills/tencent-bigdata/sub-skills/HDFS/hdfs-basic-operations) · ★1)
- **hdfs-cluster-load-diagnose**: 当用户咨询 HDFS 集群负载问题时使用此 skill。优先从用户问题中提取时间范围，通过 do-bigdata CLI 调用 get_cluster_load_info_by_time API 获取指定时段内各 DataNode 的 Xceiver 连接数分布以及 NameNode 的 RPC CallQueueLength 请求负载情况；若用户未指定时间，则回退调用 get_cluster_load_info 获取最近 30 分钟的负载数据。据此从 DataNode 和 NameNode 两个维度分析集群是否存在负载瓶颈，并给出优化建议。 ([TunoC2C/workbuddy-experts](https://github.com/TunoC2C/workbuddy-experts/tree/HEAD/plugins/ab-experiment-analyst/skills/tencent-bigdata/sub-skills/HDFS/hdfs-cluster-load-diagnose) · ★1)
- **hdfs-data-management**: Use when running MetaCacheSpark build or query jobs on a Hadoop/Spark cluster and the reference database or classification results must be shared across Spark executors via HDFS rather than a workstation-local filesystem. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hdfs-data-management))
- **hdfs-distributed-data-management**: Use when running BiSpark or a similar Spark/Hadoop pipeline over large FASTQ/A read sets (e.g. GSE80911 WGBS data at 10M-200M reads) where reference indices and inputs must be accessible from every slave node. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hdfs-distributed-data-management))
- **hdfs-distributed-sequence-partitioning**: Use when reference genome collections are too large for a single-machine k-mer/minhash database build and input FASTA sequences plus taxonomy already reside in HDFS on a Hadoop cluster with Spark 2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hdfs-distributed-sequence-partitioning))
